## 2026-09-22 Phase 536: Original page bookmark parity

- Implemented the original per-page bookmark as an LWW register end to end — a data-fidelity gap: Harmony previously deferred `BOOKMARK_UNSUPPORTED` on CreatePage/ModifyPage/bundle ops carrying `bookmarked` and never decoded ModifyPage field 3, so imported bookmark state was silently dropped. Original evidence: `oz9` (UNBOOKMARKED=0/BOOKMARKED=1) written via CreatePage field 3 (`haj.a`, default UNBOOKMARKED) and ModifyPage field 3 (`u5j.s/t` → `r0j.a`); `ge8.k()`/`ln2.k()` map unknown bytes to UNBOOKMARKED; `wz9.java:42` materializes `bookmarked` from the register winner; the content-manager toggle (`de2.m` → `ae2` case 0) is a multi-select op — any selected unbookmarked page → all BOOKMARKED, else all UNBOOKMARKED — with `md.java` fill/outline thumbnail icons and `nd2` ALL/BOOKMARKS/NOTES filter chips.
- Schema v70: `bookmarked INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` added to `page_info`, `original_deleted_page`, and `page_delete_checkpoint`; new `original_page_bookmark_winner` winner table (page-sequence identity PK, winner identity, `CHECK (0|1)`, CASCADE) mirroring the position/visibility/background registers; the migration seeds winners from `original_page_identity`.
- CreatePage drops the bookmark defer, seeds the winner per created page with op identity, and materializes `page_info.bookmarked`. ModifyPage decodes `hasBookmarked`/`bookmarked` and `applyBookmark` performs winner-read → LWW compare → winner-write + materialization on both live and archived pages. `encodeOriginalModifyPageBookmark` writes field 3 (4-aligned page vector, zero vtable entries for absent fields). The bundle path tracks `bookmarked`/`bookmarkWinner` in `BootstrapPageState`, inserts winners on fresh bootstrap, reconciles via `applyBootstrapBookmarks` on both fresh and existing-mapping paths, and validates winner rows in `existingMappingMatches`/`hasExistingMapping`.
- Runtime: `PageInfo.bookmarked` rides clone/read/write/checkpoint/archive/restore/import/export/backup-signature paths; `PageRepository.setPageBookmarked` is dual-path (original pages → `persistOriginalPageBookmark` op flow with persistent history; local pages → direct column update). UI: `PageManagerBar` shows a 🔖 indicator for the current page plus a toggle button and compact-menu entry behind the fail-closed lease guards; `NotePage.toggleCurrentPageBookmark` runs through `runPageOperation`.
- Deliberate exclusion: `bookmarked` is a mutable register, so it stays out of `PageStructureOpCodec`, `samePage`, `samePageInfo`, and `changedPages` — otherwise a bookmark flip between delete-push and redo would fail checkpoint verification; checkpoint/archive columns still carry it for faithful restore.
- Added ADR-0508, a Harmony evidence file (oz9/ge8/ln2/haj/u5j/ae2/de2/md/nd2 chain), a Chinese report, and `d02-original-page-bookmark-parity.mjs` (50 assertions). Existing anchors legitimately updated: page-bar fail-closed callback count 7→9, null-title replay `assertEqual(69)`→70. Full Desktop Replays passed 431/431; `note@default` build succeeded with 0 errors; no emulator, VM, device, or Hypium was started; T-042 remains the final Goal task. Leftovers (registered): no thumbnail grid → multi-select toggle degenerates to current-page single-select, BOOKMARKS filter chip not portable yet, icon adapted as a text glyph.

The rh9 event family (ti9 dispatch) is fully recovered — `xg9` OnAddColorClick(toolType,index), `bh9` OnDeleteColorClick(pb4), `ah9` OnColorWellValueChange(wellId,wellIndex,newColor), `yg9` OnBrushColorClick; `wj9` case 17 writes the wheel editor's color back into a well, `jm3` is the '+' entry, `ys2` the per-tool menu, `tb4` densifies trayIndex after delete.
- Harmony landing: `EditorViewModel.addFavoriteColorWell()` appends a well at `favoriteColors.length` seeded with `brushColor`; `ColorPickerView` gains a long-press context menu on each well ("Set to current color" = ah9 write path, "Delete" = bh9 + tb4 reindex) plus a dashed '+' tile (xg9); all new callbacks fail closed under `photoImportLeaseActive`; new `add_a_color`/`well_set_current_color` strings in both locales.
- Added ADR-0511, a Harmony evidence file, a Chinese report, and `d02-original-color-well-edit.mjs` (24 assertions). Full Desktop Replays passed 434/434; `note@default` and `note@ohosTest` builds succeeded with 0 errors; no emulator, VM, device, or Hypium was started. Leftovers (registered): well-write sources the current brush color rather than a nested wheel (wj9→ah9 simplified), delete entry converged to the well long-press menu.

## 2026-09-22 Phase 540: Original folder decoration tabs + categorized emoji picker

- Implemented the Phase 533 leftover: the original folder dialog's decoration section is a flag-gated (`LIBRARY_FOLDER_EMOJI_PICKER`) Color|Emoji tab pair (`hq4`), not always-stacked rows. `gaj` renders the tab row (`q31` over `hq4.M`), initializes Emoji when an emoji is set (`fsi.T`), and switches content by ordinal (Color → 6-col palette `d()`, Emoji → categorized grid `a()`); `wm2` case 0 makes decoration exclusive — switching to Color clears the emoji. `du3` fixes nine categories (Smileys…Flags) with `md` `emojis_*` strip icons; emoji data itself lives in a bundled Realm `EmojiInfo` (`ru3`) and is not recoverable.
- Harmony landing: `NameDialog` gains `decorationTab`/`emojiCategory` state, a segmented tab row (`decorationTabButton`), Color-tab palette (unchanged), and an Emoji tab with a nine-icon category strip (du3 order, representative glyphs) + ∅ clear tile + 7-column scrollable grid over `FOLDER_EMOJI_CATEGORIES` (curated 16–23 per category). Switching to Color clears `selectedEmoji` (wm2 parity); the folder row now renders emoji-first with the color dot as fallback (inference consistent with exclusive decoration). New `decoration_*`/`emoji_category_0..8` strings in both locales.
- Added ADR-0512, a Harmony evidence file, a Chinese report, and `d02-original-folder-emoji-picker.mjs` (39 assertions). Two anchors in `d02-original-folder-customization-parity.mjs` legitimately evolved (PRESETS→CATEGORIES). Full Desktop Replays passed 435/435; `note@default` and `note@ohosTest` builds succeeded with 0 errors; no emulator, VM, device, or Hypium was started. Leftovers (registered): curated emoji subset vs bundled Realm data, glyph icons vs vector `emojis_*`, picker search omitted, row precedence inferred.

## 2026-09-22 Phase 542: Original jump-to-page parity

- Original evidence: `n8.java` case 20 wraps the `page_indicator` text in the `m18.K` clickable modifier chain (click lambda `g89` opens the dialog); `ke1.java` composes `jump_to_title` + `jump_to_page_label`; `n8.java` case 21 parses input via `svd.p0` and enables Go iff `1 <= n <= pageCount`. Harmony previously rendered a non-interactive `current / total` text.
- Harmony landing: the indicator gains a fail-closed `onClick` (busy/lease guard, matching sibling callbacks) that opens `JumpToPageDialog` (CustomDialogController, autoCancel). The dialog's number input defaults to the current page; `parseInt` + whole-string comparison replicates `svd.p0`, and Go is enabled iff `1 <= n <= pageCount` with a second range check on click. `onJumpToPage` applies `currentPageIndex` under the same guard set as prev/next navigation.

Full Desktop Replays passed 437/437; `note@default` and `note@ohosTest` builds succeeded with 0 errors; no emulator, VM, device, or Hypium was started.

## 2026-09-22 Phase 543: Original note-open failure dialogs

- Harmony fix: `renderCenterPath` no longer applies `styleMap[0].backingDashPhase` as `lineDashOffset` — that was the open assumption flagged by ADR-0012. Sliced/imported strokes with nonzero phase no longer render a shifted pattern.

## 2026-09-22 Phase 575: Audit 3.3 obfuscated-class resolution (four closures)

- `fc0` impl = `hc0` (SingleAttribute): a()=altitudeAngle, b()/c()=azimuthUnitVectorX/Y, d()=force, e()=strokeWidth; `gc0` is the lerping wrapper produced by `ic0.J(ow5)`. A-15 (angleDiff shares sizeFactor's field) and A-17 (offset direction = stylus azimuth unit vector with (1,0) fallback) verified already-aligned in `PencilSplatGenerator` — closed, no code change.
- `f92.c(d,m,b,dv)` = `(d·m+b)/dv` affine map; `xaa.b` calls it with (1.0,0.0,1.0) = identity — A-19's "missing opacity clamp" never existed. Closed.
- `dd4.d()` = `clamp(·,2.6,18)`; `ms1.java:180` tolerance = `0.5/(((clamp(w·zoom,2.6,18)−2.6)/15.4)·1.5+1)/zoom` — no Math.log; Harmony `computeOriginalFitTolerance` is verbatim. A-07 closed.
- Undo depth: `nnf` uses unbounded Compose snapshot lists (`ekd`); session undo is `n1d.X0`→`tzc`/`nzc` CRDT op replay with no numeric cap in the Java layer (`tzc.a0` = 2s coalesce window). R-32 closed: original unbounded; Harmony's 128-action + 32MB dual budget recorded as an intentional divergence.
- Added ADR-0546, evidence, Chinese report, `d02-original-attribute-accessor-resolution.mjs` (15 assertions). Docs-only; suite 470 green; dual HAP 0 errors.

## 2026-09-22 Phase 577: Original shape-recognition override arbitration (e5d.b)

- `e5d.b` runs two override rules between argmax and the >0.2 accept gate: a winning LINE defers to the first ELLIPSE candidate > 0.3; a winning POLYGON with `b16.h()` (min/max edge ratio < 0.25) defers to the first candidate > 0.3, then specifically to a circle ELLIPSE (`m06.h==m06.i`) > 0.3.
- Harmony `ShapeDetector` gains DetectionResult arbitration metadata (kind/closed/circle/unevenEdges), `hasUnevenEdges` (closed rings count the wrap edge), and `applyOriginalOverride` executing both rules in original order; constants 0.3/0.25 match e5d/b16.
- `s16` decoded: I=LINE J=ARROW K=POLYGON L=ELLIPSE M=BEZIERGON.

Verification: dedicated fixture 17/17; full replay suite 472 green; dual HAP 0 errors.

- Phase 594: outside-block tap while text editing commits + deactivates

- Phase 595: selection menu STYLE opens the selection style surface —

- Phase 596: selection overlay corner-handle free transform —
  htc.e(commit,translate,scale,rotation,pivot)/qpi.b/fvb.f: the original
  corner drag = uniform scale about the pivot + rotation accumulated
  about the new rect center (z preview/commit). Harmony's overlay only
  drew a dashed frame; scaleSelected/rotateSelected were dead code.
  Added four corner handles (12vp, 22vp hit radius, hidden in
  deselectMode/lease); corner hit precedes the inside-drag branch and
  starts a selectionResize session (anchor=opposite corner, base matrix
  snapshot, gated on positionLocked); each frame scale=distance ratio,
  θ=angular displacement about the anchor → resizeSelected rebuilds
  R(scaledCenter)·S(anchor)·base; drop reuses the selectionDrag
  single TRANSFORM_ELEMENTS undo + cancel snapshot restore.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 24/24; note@default build clean.

- Phase 597: selection overlay rotation handle —

- Phase 602: text-editor suspend semantics (pke/NoneActive) — corrects

- Phase 605: LOCK/UNLOCK clears even on a no-op single-element
  selection — the dhb case18/19 itc branch runs fvb.a()

- Phase 606: two-phase ±5 hit tolerance — fu1.e queries the exact
  point first, then retries inside a ±5 rect with a radius-5 query
  circle, topmost in z-order; tolerance is in world/page units
  (fu1.j normalizes by element scale: local tolerance = 5/maxScale;
  block half-extents +fB, shape stroked band + circle, ink band +
  circle). Harmony tested the exact point only, so presses within 5
  of an edge were judged misses. topmostPageElementIdAt now runs
  exact→tol5 via hitOrderedElementIdAt; blocks expand via
  expandLocalBounds(worldTol→local by /scale), strokes/shapes add
  worldTol to the world-space hit radius; tapeIdsAtPoint falls back
  to the two-phase topmost tape singleton when the exact collection
  is empty (xtc.b setX1-empty fallback). Correction: the first cut
  treated 5 as element-local; fu1.j's scale normalization evidence
  showed it is world-space — fixed.

  builds clean.
- Phase 607: selection-region ∩ element geometry — fu1.f/g uh5 arm
  selects when the selection polygon intersects actual element
  geometry (ink stroked path via jy0.e, shape fill ∪ stroked band via
  z8a.b, block local rect), not the bounds center. Harmony used
  bbox∩rect for RECTANGLE and bounds-center-in-polygon for LASSO —
  edge-grazing strokes/shapes were missed and empty bbox corners
  were falsely selected. Added strokeIntersectsSelectionPath
  (sample-in-polygon ∨ segment-edge distance ≤ half-width),
  selectionPathHitsShape (vertex-in-polygon ∨ fill/band ∩ polygon),
  and a strokeHit injection on finalizeSelection; blocks keep
  polygon∩rect.

  d shares element.fontSize's unit domain and -24 ports literally.
  applyCodeBlockFace writes max(base-24,1) for CODE_BLOCK chars without
  explicit run fontSize; new paragraphFontSize feeds 6 line-metric sites
  (ADR-0704). Fixture 37/37, full suite in commit, dual HAP.

- Phase 753: eyedropper vp/px sample fix — same
  LengthMetricsUnit.DEFAULT=vp defect class as Phase 750:
  the old code fed vp*density touch coords into a vp-interpreted
  getImageData (~2.75x offset), used the vp edge as the physical-px
  row stride, and declared a PixelMap whose size mismatched the
  buffer (malformed loupe). Fix: region edge 24/density vp (keeps
  the original nui 24-physical-px neighborhood), ImageData real
  dims as stride/PixelMap size, touch offset (xVp-sx)*density
  (ADR-0701). Fixture 30/30, full suite in commit, dual HAP.

  ahg.S=isShown&&panelInTopHalf → u49 chromeFlip/toolboxFlip
  scaleY±1 animation — control-bar chrome mirrors vertically when
  the panel sits in the top half. Ported: clamp constants 48/336 +

- Phase 751: Zoom window edge auto-scroll (bfg) — the original
  mounts a withFrameNanos frame loop on the overlay: while a window
  gesture is pressed and the pointer enters the canvas 30dp left/right
  edge band, the viewport auto-pans (100dp/s in-band, 500dp/s past
  the edge) and the source window follows (scrollBy(-iY0) +
  setSourceRect(rect+iY0/zoom), horizontal only). Ported: per-move
  edge eval + 16ms setInterval frame loop panBy+sourceRect coupling +
  unified stop in endZoomWindowDrag (ADR-0699). a76 rect frame is
  ambiguous — classic past-edge acceleration approximation.
  Fixture 112/112, full suite in commit, dual HAP re-verified.
- Phase 750: Zoom source-window overlay (gfg/dfg/bfg) — the original

  iu1.b(0.08f) 8% scrim outside the window, zoom_outline rounded
  frame (bottom-left gap), and a bottom-left handle chip composited
  from zoom_fill/overlay/highlight/shadow; efg→dfg drives in-window
  drag setSourceRect and knob-drag resizeSourceRect (rect+magnification
  coupled, ahg.i mask=51 writes both). Ported: screen-space overlay
  in renderFrame tail + top-level onCanvasTouch routing (original
  pointerInput wraps the page) + move/resize/jump gestures +
  onDisAppear/cancel cleanup (ADR-0698). Also fixes Phase 747 vp2px
  defect (ctx is vp drawing space; stroke/rect/advance values were
  inflated ~2.75x). dfg/ffg undecompilable — hit areas and mag
  bounds are approximations. Fixture 103/103, full suite in commit,
  dual HAP re-verified.

  xgb) builds the yn2 CREATE_RECORDING entity, and te0 variant 0 emits
  a single op with no page. Harmony appends .mp3/.mp4/.aac/.wav/.aiff/
  .m4a to the picker; IMPORTED_AUDIO_SUFFIX_MIME mirrors the nj3 table
  (audio/* fallback); bytes stage under assets/pending/audio_import_*.tmp
  (persist's finally deletes it); AVMetadataExtractor fdSrc reads the
  duration (0 on failure); under importMutex, createNoteWithMeta
  (0 pages, hasRecordings=true) + persistCapturedOriginalRecording
  (startTime=now, endTime=now+duration). Divergences: original
  startTime=0L -> Harmony records the import instant (local writer
  requires >0); .mp4 imports as audio/mp4 per nj3. ADR-0622;
  dedicated 23/23, full suite 540/540, dual HAP 0 errors.
- Phase 654: standalone text file import (dv5 default branch / dhj.p0) —
  the original dv5 copies the .txt (nj3.txt, the only plain-text import
  type) to a temp file, reads it via tf4.x0(file,UTF-8), deletes the
  temp, and wraps it in tu5(string); yq8.f routes tu5 to dhj.p0:
  non-empty -> te0 (haj.a UNBOOKMARKED Letter page + nti.g(...,0)
  register position 0 + kci.b(null,text,null) f46 entity journaled as
  haa.INSERT_STRING); empty -> log + dca.c no-op. Harmony appends .txt
  to the picker filters; importTextFromBytes decodes UTF-8, fails closed
  on empty text; under importMutex, createNoteWithMeta + addImportedPage
  (Letter->mm, PLAIN) + saveElements single TEXT; buildImportedTextElement
  = textOrigin{0,0} + blockWidth 612pt + line-measured height
  (max(40,lines*25+13)) + insets 5/3/5/10 + font 17 black +
  textBlockWorldBounds. Documented approximation: the original carries
  no explicit placement (cde default register); Harmony materializes
  page-top + page-width + measured height. ADR-0621; dedicated 25/25,
  full suite 539/539, dual HAP 0 errors.
- Phase 653: standalone image file import (cv5 image branch / dhj.S) —
  the original vuh.b decodes encoded dims, swaps axes by EXIF for the
  intrinsic ep5, keeps bytes+mime when <=3000px, and rewrites oversized
  images via power-of-two inSampleSize + createScaledBitmap + baked
  rotation to WEBP_LOSSY q85; qu5(file,w,h,mime) routes via yq8.f to
  dhj.S which, on the m09.b Letter 612x792pt page, fits via bvh.a
  min(1, 0.8*page/img) (f5=null, no 320pt cap), centers with ip5 at
  scale=fitW/srcW, and kp5 emits CREATE_BLOCK IMAGE/SQUARE/PIXEL_ALIGN.
  Harmony widens the picker with .png/.jpg/.jpeg/.webp/.gif/.heif/
  .heic/.tif/.tiff suffix dispatch; prepareImportedImageBytes mirrors
  vuh.b through originalImageOrientedDimensions +
  planOriginalImageDownscale (>3000: uniform factor scaling + packToData
  webp q85, intrinsic taken from the packed product); buildImported-
  ImageElement frounds every step of bvh.a/ip5 (transform=[s,0,x,0,s,y],
  block=intrinsic); importMutex wraps createNoteWithMeta +
  storeImportedOriginalAsset + addImportedPage(Letter->mm, PLAIN) +
  saveElements single IMAGE. Recorded divergence: oversized EXIF-rotated
  images keep encoded orientation in the rewritten WebP (no EXIF in
  container; intrinsic stays self-consistent). ADR-0620; dedicated 34/34,
  full suite 538/538, dual HAP 0 errors.
- Phase 652: standalone PDF file import (jv5 PDF branch) — the original
  i58.c picker accepts */* then jv5 dispatches by sniffed MIME:
  o88(nj3.pdf) descriptor → fca.f PDFTron open → su5(r8d doc + page
  list) → nv5; one asset per document, one wz9 register per page.
  Harmony widens fileSuffixFilters to ['.note','.pdf'] (other original
  types stay fail-closed) and sniffs %PDF- magic upstream of the ZIP
  path; PDFKit loadDocument is path-only, so bytes stage under
  assets/pending and are removed after parse; getPageCount is capped
  at 10000 (the sw9 cropBoxes flatvector bound) and every page's pt
  dims pass pagePixelSize before any write; under importMutex,
  createNoteWithMeta + storeImportedOriginalAsset(application/pdf,
  sha512) + addImportedPage×N materialize a shared whole-document
  sw9 register (pagesConsumed=N/pageOffset=0/cropBoxes[N]/
  pageInAsset=i, FIT_AND_CROP_BOX) with originalPageInAsset=i, PLAIN
  template and inferPaperSize(pt→mm); failures run removeFailedImport.
  ADR-0619; fixture 32/32, full suite 537/537, dual HAP clean.
- Phase 651: batch history merge (PAGE_BATCH cross-page grouping) —
  each ae2 batch variant folds all selected pages' ops into one list and
  calls x82.I once (x82.I wraps the whole list as pq1(12,list) → a single
  m1d.v0 apply = one undo step). Harmony adds
  HistoryCoalesceTrack.PAGE_BATCH plus begin/endPageBatch windows
  (shared actionTime as monotonic batch identity; restored entries raise
  the batch clock); peekGroup merges PAGE_BATCH entries by
  noteId+track+actionTime across pages; performHistory applies+commits
  one child per pass and continuePageBatch drives the rest (cross-page
  children ride the pendingHistoryDirection navigation resume);
  dispatchPageSelectionAction wraps its whole switch; only the two
  batch-reachable writer gates relax (clearOriginalPageContent,
  commitOriginalDuplicatePageContent). ADR-0618; fixture 25/25, full
  suite 536/536, dual HAP clean.
- Phase 650: panel ops keep the viewed page + grouped batch
  duplicate — ae2/e2 contain zero qd2 references (page ops never
  write currentPageIndex), and de2.j scans the page list backwards
  for the last selected page as the single m1d.c0 insert anchor.
  Harmony: addPageAt/duplicatePageAt/pasteCopiedPageAt no longer jump
  to the new page — the viewed page is re-anchored by pageId
  (also removes a transient findIndex -1 assignment in addPageAt);
  batch duplicate now routes capturePageCopyPayload +

  zd2-v0 opacity caveat and batch-history merge stay registered in
  ADR-0617. Fixture 13/13, full suite 535/535, dual HAP clean.
- Phase 649: persisted page clear — original fd2 case1 -> de2.n ->
  ae2 variant 1 (collects j.m(page).keySet() per page -> u5j.l
  DELETE_ENTITIES -> journaled x82.I). Harmony adds
  StrokePersistence.clearOriginalPageContent: all page elements plus
  fully-covered groups are visibility-deleted via
  ORIGINAL_DELETE_ENTITIES, a DELETE_ELEMENTS companion carries the
  action history -> PERSISTED_PAGE_MUTATIONS (undo restores via
  navigate + replay); the current page keeps the canvas
  clearPageSignal path. Cell-menu Clear is unlocked for any page and
  the selection-bar Clear now accepts any non-empty selection —
  closing the ADR-0614/0615 fail-closed entries. Fixture 19/19,
  full suite 534/534, dual HAP clean.
- Phase 648: page-overview multi-select — original qd2.isSelecting/
  selectedPageIds + tfh toolbar + dg2 multi-page clipboard. Cell menu

  Select all = filtered set, Deselect/Done = de2.u() three-field reset;
  batch Copy/Duplicate/Delete/Cut/Paste/Bookmark via

  batch history journals per page — registered in ADR-0615. Fixture
  31/31, full suite 533/533, dual HAP clean.
- Phase 647: page-overview cell context menu — original n9j menu order
  (Add page/Cut/Copy/Paste?/Duplicate/Rotate?/Clear/Delete; Create
  Template flag-disabled upstream) dispatched per page key via fd2.
  Harmony: cell long-press bindContextMenu + onPageAction ->
  dispatchPageContextAction; all page ops parameterized to
  …At(pageIndex) with *CurrentPage delegating; non-current snapshots
  rebuilt via persistedPageSnapshot/persistedPageCopyPlan; delete/rotate

- Phase 646: in-panel page search — r22 case24 content_manager_search
  row -> de2.l() sets isSearchActive; de2.s(query) feeds the xd9.d

- Phase 645: original page overview panel — x90.g top-bar first slot

- Phase 644: share sheet page-range selector — v6d.l page set + b7d
  partial-export check narrowed to all-pages/current-page for

- Phase 643: share sheet PDF row lit — s6d.PDF (atc case4), b7d

  never decompiled, so raster-only, page-range, multi-note merge and

- Phase 642: share sheet JPG/PNG rows lit — s6d.JPG/PNG (atc cases 6/7)

- Phase 641: original editor top-right Share entry — x90.g renders the

  tier on by default; ke1 case 15 + toprighttoolbar_share_action), opening

- Phase 640: original page bookmark toggle made undoable — ae2
  variant 0 emits u5j.s(x09, list, null, null, oz9, 6) (mask 6 writes

  so a bookmark-only diff classifies as a no-op). Aligned: new
  OpType.PAGE_BOOKMARK = 6 with an NPBK codec (single revision step +

  ModifyPage reducer advances the revision, the legacy path bumps it

- Phase 639: original cross-note page paste unlocked — mg2.b = dg2
  (CopiedPagesData{ops,pageCount}) carries no note binding, so the

  over-conservative: CREATE_BLOCK's mergeImageAssetReference and
  CREATE_PAGE's mergePdfAsset -> mergeOriginalAssetReference already
  merge note_asset.note_ids into the target note (preserving

- Phase 638: original final-page delete compensation — de2.i appends
  u5j.i(x09, size - 1, 0, 14) to the same th7 op stream when
  list2.size() - list.size() < 2 (delete + blank insert = one undoable

  and lands at the victim's slot). Harmony aligned: new
  OpType.DELETE_PAGE_COMPENSATION = 5 with DeletePageCompensationOpCodec
  (DCP1 magic, bidirectional order-pair validation, same-slot swap);
  PageRepositoryImpl.deletePageWithCompensation runs checkpoint +
  hide/physical-delete + anchored insert at before[size - 2] +
  fail-closed landing check + companion in one transaction, with

  both directions (each appends its own UNDO/REDO companion);
  restoreCheckpointedPage/physicalDeletePage/insertPageInfoAt/
  newCompensationPage extracted as shared helpers; PersistentHistory
  materializes the companion into a DeletePageAction carrying
  compensationPage (checkpoint content reused); NotePage drops the

  PageManagerBar removes the pageCount > 1 gate. Cut (Phase 636) reuses
  deleteCurrentPage and gains compensation for free. Four stale d02
  fixture pins updated. Dedicated fixture 56/56, full suite 524/524,

- Phase 637: original Add Page anchored after the selected page —
  n9j's first menu item add_page -> c.function0 -> g.function3 ->
  fd2 case 3 -> zd2 variant 0 emits u5j.i(x09, selectedIndex + 1,
  0, 14); haj.a(anchor, null, 1, UNBOOKMARKED, 16) carries a null
  nz9 (note-default background), no bookmark, single page. Harmony
  aligned: persistOriginalCreatePage(s) take an optional
  afterPageId resolved via readPageIdentity (default stays
  readTailPosition); the original add path inserts after the anchor
  and asserts index anchorIndex + 1, while the legacy path shifts
  page_index descending to make room under the UNIQUE index;
  PersistentHistory's CREATE_PAGE materializer and
  applyAddPageHistory drop the tail-position asserts (single
  insert + empty content stay invariant, existing tail ops remain
  valid); NotePage.addPage splices at currentPageIndex + 1. The
  Phase 634 evidence doc's "zd2 variant 0 = duplicate" slip is
  corrected to Add Page. Registered follow-up: de2.i auto-inserts
  a blank page when deletion would leave <2 pages, while Harmony
  still blocks last-page deletion. Dedicated fixture 26/26, full
  suite 523/523, dual HAP 0 errors.
- Phase 636: original page-level Cut / Copy / Paste — the n9j
  single-page menu orders Add Page, Cut, Copy, Paste (only while
  mg2.b != null), Duplicate, Rotate, Clear. ae2 variant 2 serializes
  the page payload via u5j.e into the process-scoped page clipboard
  mg2.b = dg2 (CopiedPagesData { ops, pageCount }); variant 3 does
  the same then applies the de2.i delete ops (same journal as
  Delete Pages); Paste replays through lg2/m1d.c0 producing the
  same op families as Duplicate. Harmony aligned: new
  rendering/OriginalPageClipboard.ets holds the in-memory
  CopiedPagePayload (effective nz9 + bookmark + PageCopyPlan);
  captureCurrentPageCopyPlan shares the page-local group subgraph
  builder with Duplicate; insertCopiedPage shares
  insertPageAfterAnchor (clipboard metadata replaces the live
  source page) and journals the same DUPLICATE_PAGE companion
  (the original's two gestures emit identical op streams);
  Cut = copy + existing deleteCurrentPage (DELETE_PAGE); the page
  menu gains cut/copy in original order with Paste conditionally
  rendered between Copy and Duplicate; pageClipboardVersion drives
  the gate. Fail-closed: image-bearing pages refuse cross-note
  paste (per-note asset linkage) while image-free pages may.
  Dedicated fixture 48/48, full suite 522/522, dual HAP clean
  builds in the Phase 636 report.
- Phase 635: original Duplicate Page — de2.r -> ae2 variant 5
  serializes each selected page via u5j.e (wz9.u: effective nz9 +
  l7j.c single-page PDF collapse + oz9 bookmark into ln2 field 3),
  anchors after the last selected page (de2.j) and transcodes with
  fresh identities (m1d.c0). Harmony aligned:
  persistOriginalDuplicatePage emits a source-anchored CreatePage
  carrying the full effective nz9 plus the bookmark passthrough
  (ln2 field 3 = 1); the extracted transcodeOriginalPageElements
  is shared with clipboard paste while

- Phase 633: original FIT_TO_PAGE verified as an upstream dead
  item — dsc ordinal 15 renders normally in ux9, but dispatcher
  dhb case 15 is a bare throw NotImplementedError(0)
  (dhb.java:17986): the original button is dead on arrival, so
  Harmony's omission is parity (strictly better than the
  original's crash). SelectionOverlay comment cites the stub,

  replay gained 3 assertions pinning the conclusion. No behavior

- Phase 632: stroke-renderer c5g surface audit close-out — the
  gap-list's two open leads verified as already-parity: the dash

  pool — a wrong reference): DASH {2f,1f}+BUTT/ROUND, DOTS
  {0.001f,2f}+ROUND/MITER, phase always 0, matching Harmony's
  renderCenterPath exactly; the highlighter is zx1.e(color,107)
  alpha override (0.42) with all paints SRC_OVER — not a MULTIPLY
  simplification; l96.l0 clipOut+fill is isomorphic to the
  evenodd clip+fill. Only change: two comments pzf.g -> c5g.g;
  gap-list unaudited 12 -> 11, trunk-covered 6 -> 7. No behavior
  change; related fixtures stay green.
- Phase 631: system-clipboard URI image carrier — the original
  paste gate x7j.c is ClipDescription.hasMimeType("image/*"), so
  URI-carried images are equally pasteable
  (v49.handleAddImageFromClipboard). Harmony probed only
  MIMETYPE_PIXELMAP, making text/uri image copies unpasteable.
  Aligned: probeWithHarmonyPasteboardMimeTypes gains a TEXT_URI
  branch (hasData -> getData -> first record.uri passing

  URI as source when no pixelMap record exists;
  normalizeClipboardImageUri reads via fileIo read-only with the

  normalizeOriginalImageBytes (the same normalizer as the photo

  mimeType/rewroteBytes/fileExtension/encodedWidth/encodedHeight

- Phase 630: canvas image drag-drop ingress — the original
  dr.onDrag case 3 -> ol3.P0 -> pl3 chain delivers drops to the
  canvas consumer e0f.P0, which collects every ClipData item with
  a non-null getUri() and invokes wx4.invoke(list, mp5(x,y)) with
  the drop point; empty/absent URIs or permission denial return
  false. Harmony had no onDrop/DragEvent wiring at all. Aligned:
  the root Stack gains .onDrop -> onOriginalImageDrop (same
  canStartOriginalPhotoInsert gates + DROP_ENABLED/DROP_DISABLED
  receipts); the drop point maps getWindowX/Y - Area.globalPosition
  -> screenToCanvas -> getOriginalPhotoInsertOrigin; new
  OriginalDragDropIngress extracts UDMF records
  (general.file-uri/image/pixelmap), routes extension-bearing URIs
  through importOriginalPhotos (picker-identical), decodes
  extension-less URIs fail-closed per item, and normalizes
  pixelmaps via the newly exported normalizeOriginalPixelMap
  (refactored out of the clipboard ingress). Text-field drop
  (ame) is out of scope. Dedicated 45/45; suite + dual HAP in the
  Phase 630 report.
- Phase 629: clipboard paste source zIndex preservation — the
  original's clipboard records carry z asymmetrically: only the
  ink record u6a has a serialized z field (long j), and the paste
  producer o8j.a feeds it verbatim via new xgb(u6aVar2.j) into
  u5j.g's zIndex slot; shape record ge3 has no z field and all
  u5j.j / baj.a paste producers pass null -> clientTime top.
  Harmony previously omitted zIndex for every kind, so a pasted
  999999-underlay highlighter popped to the top. Aligned:
  commitOriginalClipboardPaste gains readOriginalClipboardSourceZIndex
  — a direct original_element_z_index lookup by source elementId
  (no note/page/visible constraint; op-ids are globally unique so
  cut sources, cross-page and cross-note pastes all hit) — wired
  only for STROKE; shapes/blocks keep the null->clientTime parity;
  the z-clock guard now applies only to clientTime-fallback
  elements. Encoders gain format-completeness zIndex?: string
  params (CreateShape field 12 at offset 28; CreateBlock field 9
  via conditional objectSize +8) with only ink wired today.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 38/38; full suite and dual HAP
  clean builds recorded in the Phase 629 report.
- Phase 628: highlighter underlay z-band probe — the original
  u5j.g (CreateInk producer) probes the stroke start point when
  zIndex is absent and InkTool==HIGHLIGHTER(u16=2): outside every
  live element bounds -> zIndex=999999 underlay band (below
  clientTime ~1.7e12 content), inside -> null->clientTime (top);
  u5j.j (CreateShape) carries the same probe, and g1f generated
  highlight rects pin 999999 unconditionally. Harmony previously
  always landed highlighters at clientTime top. Aligned:
  writeOriginalCreateInk calls originalHighlighterUnderlayZIndex
  (isHighlighter gate + transform*pathPoints[0] start vs current
  bounds, inclusive); the shape side needs no probe (the source
  ink still covers the shape origin in the original -> always
  inside -> same outcome), and g1f has no Harmony ingress.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 29/29; full suite 516/516;
  note@default/note@ohosTest 0 errors.
- Phase 608: region-select excludes positionLocked — fu1.b
  (ac4.Q POSITION_LOCKED, on by default) drops jrh.a-locked

  blocks oy0.t(), lockable shapes cih.a && n5d.t(); strokes are
  never filtered; tap-select stays unfiltered so locked elements
  remain tappable for unlock. Harmony previously swept locked
  elements into lasso/rect selections. finalizeSelection now
  skips positionLocked in the four hit loops before group
  expansion; four unit-test expectations updated.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 9/9; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 609: caret lands at the tapped character — dl1 itc arm
  produces ttc(blockId, jE); uw2 case4 activates the editor via
  qke then dispatches sqa(rej.i(zn9.f world→local + layout offset
  hit)) placing the caret at the pressed position unconditionally,
  overriding the suspended session caret. Harmony only restored
  the suspended caret. New caretIndexAtPoint (rej.i /
  getOffsetForPosition parity: nearest-line y clamp + half-width
  x split); beginTextEditingAt now overrides restoreCaret with the
  tap caret after activation; link hit (tqa parity) still precedes

- Verification: dedicated fixture 12/12; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 610: copy/cut exclude position-locked elements — lg2.g
  filters ktc.h() through jrh.a under ac4.Q (POSITION_LOCKED,
  on by default): oy0 blocks t()/lockable shapes cih.a&&t() drop,
  s06 strokes never filter; bounds/copy-ops/group payload all build
  on the filtered set; dhb case1 clears selection only on success,
  all-locked → g()=null → no write no clear; case4 DUPLICATE uses
  raw ftc.q/m ids unfiltered. Harmony adds
  clipboardSelectionWithoutLocked: COPY filters before the clipboard
  write, CUT uses the filtered set as both payload and delete
  target (gg2.set semantics; delete-target choice is a fail-closed
  inference recorded in ADR-0579), DUPLICATE stays unfiltered.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 20/20; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 611: selection-drag touch-slop gate — ycj.java:99 surface
  detector (is7.onStartDrag/onTouchSlopReached/onDragMove + vo2
  finish), awaitPointerSlopOrCancellation family: onDragMove is not
  dispatched until displacement passes scaledTouchSlop and the
  in-slop displacement is consumed; release before slop is a tap.
  Harmony dragged from the first move delta. New
  selectionGestureSlopAdvance accumulates screen-space |dDelta| and
  crosses at 8px (registered adaptation); the first post-slop drag
  event applies only the residual (|accum|-8)/zoom; up-path deltas
  are skipped when never crossed — press-release is a clean tap with
  no undo. Resize gated identically; lasso keeps its size>=3 gate.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 23/23; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 612: deselect-mode tap whitelists the hit test — dl1:105-107
  xtc.a(jE, ftc.g) → fu1.e set param restricts hits to the selected
  id set across both phases (exact + tolerance); unselected elements
  never occlude selected ones beneath. Harmony took the global
  topmost hit then rejected unselected ids — an unselected element
  on top blocked the covered selected element. Added an optional
  whitelist param to hitOrderedElementIdAt/topmostPageElementIdAt;
  deselectTargetIdsAt passes the selected set; other callers stay
  unfiltered.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 13/13; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 613: text-edit undo coalesce tracks — vnf.d:56-82 assigns each
  qnf entry's track from uq9.m() (haa op type): INSERT_CHAR/
  INSERT_STRING/MODIFY_STYLE/MODIFY_PARAGRAPH_STYLE/CLEAR_STYLE →
  INSERT_TEXT (2s), REMOVE_CHAR/REMOVE_CHARS → REMOVE_TEXT (2s),
  CREATE_INK → CREATE_INK (10ms), everything else null. peekGroup
  already mirrored the grouping semantics, but Harmony commits whole
  editing sessions as REPLACE_ELEMENT actions that were always NONE —
  rapid same-direction edits across sessions could not merge. Added
  coalesceTrackFor: single-block stroke-free REPLACE diffs richText by
  common prefix/suffix — added-only → INSERT_TEXT, removed-only →
  REMOVE_TEXT, mixed → NONE (fail-closed); ADD_ELEMENT ≈ CREATE_BLOCK
  stays NONE.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 19/19; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 614: copy/cut drop unresolvable groups — lg2.g:107-168 runs
  each selected cqc group through lg2.c:51-105 recursive resolution:
  the member closure must lie entirely inside the jrh.a-filtered set;
  one locked member fails the whole group (excluded from arrayList3/5,
  so it pastes ungrouped), while unlocked members still copy loose.
  Harmony forwarded selectedGroupIds unfiltered, so
  copyOriginalGroupGraph nulled the entire preparation — COPY wrote
  nothing, CUT deleted nothing. Added copyResolvableGroupIds
  (kept/resolved→true, failed/visiting/non-group/empty→false); COPY and
  CUT consume kept.groupIds. DUPLICATE keeps raw ftc.q/m ids.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 17/17; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 615: DUPLICATE is ungated (decode correction) — dhb case
  numbers ARE dsc ordinals (DUPLICATE=3, GROUP=4); case3 dispatches
  vsc.I=1 -> lg2.b (copy + in-place paste-apply for any ktc, no
  count gate); the A1>=2 gate in case4 belongs to GROUP (kk9).
  Harmony's unconditional DUPLICATE was already correct — the
  mistakenly added gate was reverted; GROUP keeps its existing
  authoringMembers>=2 gate.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 12/12; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 616: UNGROUP only for single-group selections — dhb
  case5:17655-17672 dispatches wsc only for gtc; the ftc branch
  resolves the first group's members then returns without dispatch
  (mixed selections with one group are a dead path). Harmony's old
  gate (selectedGroupIds==1) allowed mixed selections. New gate:
  authoring members == [the selected group id] exactly; the runtime
  guard mirrors it fail-closed.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 11/11; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 617: LOCK/UNLOCK gate — xsc.k:216-240 resolves flat
  ktc.h() members; any non-m4d member (stroke/oy0 block) -> null
  dead path; an n5d shape member must exist; direction = all
  locked -> UNLOCK. itc single elements lock via u5j.n/x
  (oy0/m4d); a lone stroke produces an empty op. The old gate
  counted strokes in selectedCount and showed LOCK on a lone
  stroke. New gate: allMembersAreShapes (entities + group leaves
  all shapes) OR a single lockable element; execution merges
  group leaves into the effective shape set, keeping the
  t()!=z15 per-shape filter.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 12/12; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 618: DUPLICATE paste offset — lg2.b pastes at cg2.c()
  (selection rect center) + cg2.a() = min(width*0.1, 30) on both
  axes (fi3.b = rect center). Harmony shared the plain center
  with PASTE so duplicates overlapped exactly. duplicateSelected
  now derives the page-unit width via (right-left)/zoom, nudges
  by min(width*0.1,30); PASTE unchanged.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 12/12; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 619: rotated selection hit-test — yxi.e rotates the test
  point by -g() about the unrotated rect (htc.a()) center, then
  axis-aligned containment (four dl1 call sites). Harmony's
  selectionRect is the rotated elements' screen AABB so its corners
  false-hit. Added singleSelectedUnrotatedScreenRect (itc scope:
  clone minus rotationRadians -> unrotated bounds -> screen rect;
  shape branch narrows to ELLIPSE) + pointInSelectionRect (the -theta
  inverse-rotation equivalent of y18.c); all four inside-checks
  switched. Multi/group selections fall back to AABB (no shell-level
  rotation register). lg2.f's htc region-rect offset is not
  expressible in the model — verified no behavioral gap.
- Verification: dedicated fixture 17/17; note@default/note@ohosTest
  builds clean.
- Phase 620: recent-interaction menu gate — g39.b() = within 200ms
  of the last mark; g1f marks on selection-emptied ktc events
  (case4) and every r5f surface change (default); yqa's long-press
  paste menu silently returns inside the window (z=false branch).
  Harmony's bindContextMenu had no suppression. Aligned:
  clearSelectionWithRegisterReset + two deselect sites stamp
  lastSelectionClearTime; applyActiveState stamps toolChangedAt on
  real tool changes; ClipboardPasteContextMenu emits no MenuItem
  inside the gate (no veto hook — empty Menu does not pop).
  ej9 case19's wtc suppression is inexpressible (no l51 bus).
- Verification: dedicated fixture 14/14; note@default/note@ohosTest
